960 entries, worker-fast to workflow_scope_compliance_reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

One bounded task: bulk reading and summarising, log or test-output digestion, searches, renames, doc or ticket-comment edits, and any ticket end to end - features and fixes across modules, bugs from a user's report, tests, lint and type fixes, dependency bumps, PR fix rounds; and find-out work: an investigation, a root-cause hunt, triage, research, a read-only review. Never design. ([golding-software/agent-work-kit](https://github.com/golding-software/agent-work-kit/blob/HEAD/claude/plugins/work-room/agents/worker-haiku.md))

- **worker-high**: Sonnet 5.5 at high effort for routine, clearly specified execution that does not need extra-high effort. - **worker-high**: Effort shim for /orchestrate — a sub-agent that inherits the parent's tools and runs on whatever model the dispatch names. ([davekim917/bootstrap](https://github.com/davekim917/bootstrap/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestrate/agents/worker-high.md) · ★1)
- **worker-high**: High-effort routed worker for fable-router. Tricky debugging, cross-file refactors, retry after failed validation, and consequential independent second-opinion review. Combine with the Agent tool's model parameter to pick the model tier. ([KyongSik-Yoon/fable-router](https://github.com/KyongSik-Yoon/fable-router/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-high.md) · ★1)
- **worker-high**: High-difficulty implementation worker for LARGE changes - new modules, cross-module refactors, concurrency/security/migration work, or complex problems with ONE clear goal. ([cameleonh/omo-adaptive](https://github.com/cameleonh/omo-adaptive/blob/HEAD/packages/omo-claude/agents/worker-high.md))

- **worker-light**: Closed-spec implementation (Sonnet): executes an explicit spec — target files or area, the approach or a pattern to follow, and a checkable done condition. Makes no design decisions; on ambiguity, a spec gap, or findings that contradict the spec, it stops and reports instead of choosing. Use for bounded changes where the approach is already decided: applying a specified fix, extending an existing pattern, mechanical refactors, test repair with a known target. When the task needs investigation or an approach chosen, use worker-deep. ([viktortnk/afford-claude-fable](https://github.com/viktortnk/afford-claude-fable/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-light.md) · ★2)
- **worker-light**: Лёгкий помощник общего назначения для CEO: найти, прочитать, разобрать, пересказать, отсортировать, простой скрипт по готовому рецепту. Не считает выводы и не решает; пишет только в scratchpad сессии, не в проект. Работа, где нужно думать (разбор данных, сверка, скрипт с логикой), — не ему, а тикет Инженеру или Исследователю. ([Gogi213/role-play-vibing](https://github.com/Gogi213/role-play-vibing/blob/HEAD/agents/worker-light.md) · ★1)
- **worker-light**: Sonnet 5.5 の軽量実装ワーカー。機械的な作業（改名、参照の更新、文書の書き換え、既存の形に倣った単純なテストの追加）を 1 件ずつ完遂する。設計判断やルール検査テストの新規作成は worker（Opus）に任せる。 ([d-kanai/ai-only-sample](https://github.com/d-kanai/ai-only-sample/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-light.md))

- **worker-liveness**: **Off-loop ticker** (`worker-liveness-ticker.ts` + `worker-liveness-thread.ts`): `start()` spawns a `node:worker_threads` ticker that renews the lease on its own thread, so a CPU-bound synchronous handler on the main loop can't starve it, but only when a second connection on the **same engine** as the runtime's is available (`planLivenessConnection()`, #4978). PostgreSQL: the thread builds its own ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/jobs/agents/worker-liveness.md) · ★7)
